	<script>window.rltUpsellState = { trigger: '', type: '' };
window.rltOpenUpsell = function(opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    var type = opts.type === 'premium' ? 'premium' : 'signin';
    window.rltUpsellState.type = type;
    window.rltUpsellState.trigger = opts.trigger || '';
    var headline = opts.headline || (type === 'premium' ? 'Upgrade to Premium' : 'Sign in to save your stars');
    var body = opts.body || (type === 'premium'
        ? 'This is a Premium feature. Become a member to unlock it.'
        : 'Your starred launches are only on this device. Sign in (free) to sync them everywhere.');
    var ctaHref = type === 'premium' ? '/premium' : '/users/signin';
    var ctaLabel = type === 'premium' ? 'Upgrade to Premium' : 'Sign in (free)';
    document.getElementById('rlt-upsell-headline').textContent = headline;
    document.getElementById('rlt-upsell-body').textContent = body;
    var cta = document.getElementById('rlt-upsell-cta');
    cta.setAttribute('href', ctaHref);
    cta.textContent = ctaLabel;
    document.getElementById('rlt-upsell-overlay').classList.add('is-open');
    document.getElementById('rlt-upsell-overlay').setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
    if (typeof _paq !== 'undefined') {
        var action = type === 'premium' ? 'PremiumUpsellShown' : 'SignInNudgeShown';
        _paq.push(['trackEvent', 'Funnel', action, window.rltUpsellState.trigger || '']);
    }
};
window.rltCloseUpsell = function(viaDismissButton) {
    document.getElementById('rlt-upsell-overlay').classList.remove('is-open');
    document.getElementById('rlt-upsell-overlay').setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    if (viaDismissButton && typeof _paq !== 'undefined') {
        var action = window.rltUpsellState.type === 'premium' ? 'PremiumUpsellDismiss' : 'SignInNudgeDismiss';
        _paq.push(['trackEvent', 'Funnel', action, window.rltUpsellState.trigger || '']);
    }
};
document.getElementById('rlt-upsell-overlay').addEventListener('click', function(e) {
    if (e.target.id === 'rlt-upsell-overlay') rltCloseUpsell(true);
});
document.getElementById('rlt-upsell-cta').addEventListener('click', function() {
    if (typeof _paq !== 'undefined') {
        var action = window.rltUpsellState.type === 'premium' ? 'PremiumUpsellClick' : 'SignInNudgeClick';
        _paq.push(['trackEvent', 'Funnel', action, window.rltUpsellState.trigger || '']);
    }
});
</script>	<script src="/res/js/lightslider.js"></script><script src="/res/js/lightgallery.js"></script><script src="/res/js/lg-video.js"></script><script src="/res/js/lg-thumbnail.js"></script><script>
var launches_populated = false;
var queryParameters = rltGetQueryParams();
var tag_text = "";
//var is_launch_day = false;
var is_launch_day = false;
// past launches are intentionally shown on ?pastOnly=1 AND ?includePast=1 —
// both must disable the client-side T+2h hiding/demotion in rltApplyAllFilters()
var is_past_only = false;
if (typeof queryParameters["filter"] == "undefined") {
	var page_num = 1;
} else {
	var page_num = 0;
}
var plint = false;
var starredLaunchIds = [];
var starredFilterActive = false;

window.onpopstate = function(event) {
	if (location.pathname != "/") {
		location.reload();
	}

	var queryParameters = rltGetQueryParams();

	$("#launch_filter").val((typeof queryParameters["filter"] == "undefined") ? "0" : queryParameters["filter"]);
	updateContentFromFilter($("#launch_filter"));

};

function updateContentFromFilter(selectElement) {
	var val = selectElement.val();
	if (val === "0" || val == "undefined" || typeof val === "undefined") {
		$("#launch_filter option:first").html("Select filter");
	} else {
		$("#launch_filter option:first").html("Clear filter");
	}
	rltDebug("updateContentFromFilter", { val: val, filterType: $("#launch_filter").find(":selected").attr("data-filtertype") });
	rltApplyAllFilters();
}

function rltRepositionInFeedAd() {
	var adEl = $("#ezoic-pub-ad-placeholder-122");
	if (!adEl.length) return;
	var visibleLaunches = $(".launchloop > div.row.launch").not(".filtered_launch");
	if (visibleLaunches.length < 5) {
		adEl.hide();
	} else {
		adEl.show();
		var fifthLaunch = visibleLaunches.eq(4);
		fifthLaunch.after(adEl);
	}
}

$("#launch_filter").change(
	function () {
			if (!launches_populated) {
				launches_populated = true;
				populate_launches();
			}

			$("div.launches.row.content").fadeOut("fast", function() {
				updateContentFromFilter($("#launch_filter"));
				$("div.launches.row.content").fadeIn("fast");		

				var selected_filter_name = $("#launch_filter").find(":selected").html().replace(/&nbsp;/gi,"");
				var selected_filter_val = $("#launch_filter").find(":selected").val();

				if (selected_filter_val != "0") 
					queryParameters["filter"] = selected_filter_val;
				else
					delete queryParameters["filter"];

				history.pushState(null, null, "?" + $.param(queryParameters));
				var title = (selected_filter_name == "Select filter" ? "" : selected_filter_name) + " Launch " + (queryParameters["pastOnly"] == "1" ? "History" : "Schedule") + (tag_text != "" ? " [" + tag_text + "]" : "") + " - RocketLaunch.Live";
				var url = "?" + $.param(queryParameters);

				document.title = title; 

				if (typeof _paq !== "undefined") {
					_paq.push(["trackEvent", title, url]);	
				}

				// Drop any includePast/pastOnly already in the URL (e.g. includePast=0)
				// so the forced value is not duplicated (mirrors the server-rendered links).
				var pastLinkParams = $.extend({}, queryParameters);
				delete pastLinkParams["includePast"];
				delete pastLinkParams["pastOnly"];
				$(".prev_launches_link").attr("href", "?" + $.param(pastLinkParams) + "&includePast=1");
				$(".launch_history_link").attr("href", "?" + $.param(pastLinkParams) + "&pastOnly=1");
				$(".tag_link").each(function() {
					var link_queryParameters = rltGetQueryParams($(this).attr("href").substring(2));
					if (selected_filter_val != 0) {
						link_queryParameters["filter"] = selected_filter_val;
					} else {
						delete link_queryParameters["filter"];
					}
					$(this).attr("href", "/?" + $.param(link_queryParameters));
				});

				rltPollLaunches();
			});

	});

function populate_launches() {
	page_num++;

	$("#loading_icon").fadeIn();

	var queryParameters = rltGetQueryParams();

	queryParameters["page"] = page_num;
	delete queryParameters["filter"];

	$.ajax({
		type:"GET",
		url: "/?"+$.param(queryParameters),
		success: function(status) {
			var launches = $(status).find(".launchloop").html();

			if (launches) {			
				launchesdata = $.parseHTML(launches);

				// need to prevent duplicates from being added
				$(status).find(".launchloop").children().each(function (key) {
					var existing = $(".launchloop > div#" + this.id);
					if (existing.length > 0) {
						var activeEmbed = existing.find("div.launch_live_embed div.tabs-content div.content.active").first();
						if (activeEmbed.length) {
							// update children except the live embed div
							var newEl = $(this);
							existing.children().not("div.launch_live_embed").each(function(index) {
								if (newEl.children().not("div.launch_live_embed").eq(index)[0]) {
									$(this).replaceWith(newEl.children().not("div.launch_live_embed").eq(index)[0].outerHTML);
								}
							});
							// update tabs
							existing.children("div.launch_live_embed").children("ul.tabs").each(function(index) {
								if (newEl.children("div.launch_live_embed").children("ul.tabs").eq(index)[0]) {
									$(this).replaceWith(newEl.children("div.launch_live_embed").children("ul.tabs").eq(index)[0].outerHTML);
								}
							});
							// update non-active panels only
							if (newEl.find("div.launch_live_embed div.tabs-content").children().length) {
								newEl.find("div.launch_live_embed div.tabs-content").children().each(function() {
									if (!(existing.find("div.launch_live_embed #" + this.id).length)) {
										existing.find("div.launch_live_embed div.tabs-content").append(this.outerHTML);
									} else if (!$("#" + this.id).hasClass("active")) {
										$("#" + this.id).replaceWith(this.outerHTML);
									}
								});
							}
							// remove panels that no longer exist
							existing.find("div.launch_live_embed div.tabs-content").children().not("div.content.active").each(function() {
								if (!newEl.find("div.launch_live_embed #" + this.id).length) {
									$(this).remove();
								}
							});
							existing.find("div.launch_live_embed ul.tabs li.tab-title a").bind("click", rltSwitchLive);
						} else {
							existing.html(this.innerHTML);
						}
					} else {
						$(".launchloop").append(this);
					}
				});

				//$(".launchloop").append(launches);

				rltDebug("populate_launches batch", { page: page_num, newLaunches: $(status).find(".launchloop").children().length });

				// Apply weather units to newly loaded launches
				rltApplyWeatherUnits();

				// Init star buttons on newly loaded launches
				rltInitStarButtons();

				// Single unified filter pass: handles dropdown, checkbox, starred, past, recentlaunch cap,
				// next-launch promotion, backgrounds, ad repositioning, and filter counts.
				rltApplyAllFilters();

				// if sort=modified is set, sort accordingly
				if (queryParameters["sort"] == "modified") {
					var launchloop_launches = $(".launchloop").children("div.row.launch");

					if (queryParameters["direction"] == "asc") {
						launchloop_launches.sort(function(a,b){
							aDate = new Date($(a).find(".updated.rlt_datetime").first().attr("title"));
							bDate = new Date($(b).find(".updated.rlt_datetime").first().attr("title"));

							return aDate - bDate;
						});
					} else {
						launchloop_launches.sort(function(a,b){
							aDate = new Date($(a).find(".updated.rlt_datetime").first().attr("title"));
							bDate = new Date($(b).find(".updated.rlt_datetime").first().attr("title"));

							return bDate - aDate;
						});
					}

					launchloop_launches.detach().appendTo($(".launchloop"));

				}

				// only keep loading pages if there is no filter set
				if (typeof queryParameters["filter"] == "undefined") {
					populate_launches();
				} else {
					$("#loading_icon").fadeOut();
				}
			} else {
				$("#loading_icon").fadeOut();

				//this is required because at the time of page load, the anchors are hidden, so we need to wait until the fadeIn(), then jump to the anchor again
				if (location.hash) {
					var anchor = $(location.hash).get(0);
					if (anchor) { anchor.scrollIntoView(); }
				}

				// insert upcoming launches div in the appropriate place after all launches are populated
				if (queryParameters["includePast"] == "1" && (typeof queryParameters["sort"] == "undefined" || 
						(queryParameters["sort"] == "best_date_sort" && 
							(typeof queryParameters["direction"] == "undefined" || queryParameters["direction"] == "asc")
						)
					)
					) {

						$(".launch").each(function() {
							var launch_datetime = new Date(Date.parse($(this).find(".rlt_datetime").attr("data-sortDate")));

							if (launch_datetime.getTime() > Date.now()) {							
								$("#upcoming_launches_header").insertBefore($(this)).show();

								return false; // breaks out of each
							}
						});
				}
			}

		},
		error: function(xhr,textStatus,error) {
			$("#loading_icon").fadeOut();

			//this is required because at the time of page load, the anchors are hidden, so we need to wait until the fadeIn(), then jump to the anchor again
			if (location.hash) {
				var anchor = $(location.hash).get(0);
				if (anchor) { anchor.scrollIntoView(); }
			}

			// insert upcoming launches div in the appropriate place after all launches are populated
			if (queryParameters["includePast"] == "1" && (typeof queryParameters["sort"] == "undefined" || 
					(queryParameters["sort"] == "best_date_sort" && 
						(typeof queryParameters["direction"] == "undefined" || queryParameters["direction"] == "asc")
					)
				)
				) {

					$(".launch").each(function() {
						var launch_datetime = new Date(Date.parse($(this).find(".rlt_datetime").attr("data-sortDate")));

						if (launch_datetime.getTime() > Date.now()) {							
							$("#upcoming_launches_header").insertBefore($(this)).show();

							return false; // breaks out of each
						}
					});
			}
		}
	});

	rltUpdateLaunchFilterCounts();
	rltUpdateTimes($("select.timezone_select").val());
}

	var filterbox = $("div#filter-box");
	$(document).on("click", "div a#filter-link", function(e) {
		if (!launches_populated && window.location.pathname == "/") {
    		launches_populated = true;

		} else {

		}

		e.preventDefault();

	});

	function rltGetSeFingerprint() {
		return $("div#specialevent-box ul.tabs li.tab-title").map(function() {
			return this.id.replace("tab_live", "");
		}).get().sort().join(",");
	}

	function rltShowSeMiniBar() {
		if ($("#se-mini-bar").length) return;
		var title = $("div#specialevent-box h1").first().text().trim() || "Live Coverage";
		var ids = rltGetSeFingerprint();
		var isLive = $("#specialevent-box ul.tabs span.live-dot").length > 0;
		$("#user_info").after(
			"<div id=\"se-mini-bar\"><a href=\"#\" id=\"se-expand-link\" data-ids=\"" + ids + "\">" +
			(isLive ? "<span class=\"live-dot\"></span> " : "") +
			$("<span>").text(title).html() +
			" <span class=\"se-expand-arrow\">&#9654;</span></a></div>"
		);
	}

	$(document).on("click", "div a#specialevent-link", function(e) {
		e.preventDefault();
		var ids = rltGetSeFingerprint();
		// Stop any playing iframes by blanking their src
		$("div#specialevent-box iframe").each(function() {
			$(this).data("se-src", $(this).attr("src")).attr("src", "");
		});
		$("div#specialevent-box").slideUp(400, function() {
			try { localStorage.setItem("se_collapsed_" + ids, "1"); } catch(err) {}
			rltShowSeMiniBar();
		});
	});

	$(document).on("click", "#se-expand-link", function(e) {
		e.preventDefault();
		var ids = $(this).data("ids");
		// Restore iframe srcs
		$("div#specialevent-box iframe").each(function() {
			var s = $(this).data("se-src");
			if (s) $(this).attr("src", s);
		});
		$("div#specialevent-box").slideDown(400);
		$("#se-mini-bar").remove();
		try { localStorage.removeItem("se_collapsed_" + ids); } catch(err) {}
	});

	// On page load: restore collapsed state if previously dismissed
	(function() {
		var box = $("div#specialevent-box");
		if (!box.length) return;
		var ids = rltGetSeFingerprint();
		try {
			if (localStorage.getItem("se_collapsed_" + ids) === "1") {
				box.hide();
				rltShowSeMiniBar();
			}
		} catch(e) {}
	})();

	function rltUpdateLaunchFilterCounts() {
		var provider_count = $("input[type=\"checkbox\"].provider-check").filter(":checked").length;
		var location_count = $("input[type=\"checkbox\"].location-check").filter(":checked").length;

		// Update counts
		$("span.selected_providers_count").html(provider_count ? provider_count : "all");
		$("span.selected_locations_count").html(location_count ? location_count : "all");
		$("span.selected_launches_count").html($("div.row.launch").not(".filtered_launch").length);
	}

$("select.timezone_select").change(
	function () {
		rltUpdateTimes($("select.timezone_select").val());
	}
);

// Detect Fahrenheit vs metric locale (global scope so rlt-main.js can call it)
var fahrenheitRegions = ["US", "BS", "KY", "PW", "FM", "MH", "GU", "PR", "VI", "AS", "MP"];
var regionMatch = (navigator.language || "").match(/[-_]([A-Z]{2})$/i);
var userRegion = regionMatch ? regionMatch[1].toUpperCase() : (navigator.language === "en" ? "US" : "");
window.rltUseMetric = (fahrenheitRegions.indexOf(userRegion) === -1);

function rltApplyWeatherUnits() {
	$(".rlt_weather").not("[data-units-applied]").each(function() {
		var $el = $(this);
		var f = parseInt($el.data("temp-f"));
		var mph = parseInt($el.data("wind-mph"));
		var condition = $el.data("condition");
		var updated = $el.data("updated");
		if (isNaN(f) || !condition) return;
		var tempStr, windStr;
		if (window.rltUseMetric) {
			tempStr = Math.round((f - 32) * 5 / 9) + "°C";
			windStr = Math.round(mph * 1.609) + " km/h";
		} else {
			tempStr = f + "°F";
			windStr = mph + " mph";
		}
		$el.find(".rlt_weather_temp").text(tempStr);
		var launchTime = $el.data("launch-time");
		var label = (launchTime && new Date(launchTime) < new Date()) ? "Conditions at launch" : "Forecast";
		$el.attr("title", label + "\n\n" + condition + "\nTemp: " + tempStr + "\nWind: " + windStr + "\n\nLast updated " + updated);
		$el.attr("data-units-applied", "1");
	});
}

function rltStarUnstarredColor() {
	var theme = localStorage.getItem("rltTheme") || "light";
	if (theme === "dark") return "#9ca3af";
	if (theme === "auto" && window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches) return "#9ca3af";
	return "#333";
}

function rltInitStarButtons() {
	rltDebug("rltInitStarButtons", { btnCount: $(".launch-star-btn").length, starredLaunchIds: starredLaunchIds.slice() });
	$(".launch-star-btn").each(function() {
		var btn = $(this);
		btn.show();
		btn.closest(".launch-star").find(".launch-star-count").show();
		var launchId = btn.closest(".launch-star").data("launch-id");
		if (starredLaunchIds.indexOf(launchId) !== -1) {
			btn.html("&#9733;").attr("title", "Unstar this launch").css({"color":"#f5c518","text-shadow":"0 0 6px rgba(245,197,24,0.6)"});
			var countEl = btn.closest(".launch-star").find(".launch-star-count");
			if (countEl.text() === "") { countEl.html("1"); }
		} else {
			btn.html("&#9734;").attr("title", "Star this launch").css({"color":rltStarUnstarredColor(),"text-shadow":"none"});
		}
	});
}

function rltToggleStar(btn) {
	var starEl = btn.closest(".launch-star");
	var launchId = starEl.data("launch-id");
	var wasStarred = starredLaunchIds.indexOf(launchId) !== -1;
	var isAnon = (signed_in_user === false);
	var matchedEls = $(".launch-star[data-launch-id=" + launchId + "]");
	rltDebug("rltToggleStar entry", {
		launchId: launchId,
		wasStarred: wasStarred,
		isAnon: isAnon,
		signedInUserState: (signed_in_user === undefined ? "undefined" : (signed_in_user === false ? "false" : "user")),
		matchedElementCount: matchedEls.length,
		starredLaunchIdsBefore: starredLaunchIds.slice()
	});

	// Optimistic update — toggle immediately
	if (wasStarred) {
		starredLaunchIds = starredLaunchIds.filter(function(id) { return id !== launchId; });
	} else {
		starredLaunchIds.push(launchId);
	}
	matchedEls.each(function(idx) {
		var el = $(this);
		var countEl = el.find(".launch-star-count");
		var currentCount = parseInt(countEl.text()) || 0;
		// Anon stars do not contribute to the server-side popularity counter;
		// only adjust the visible count for signed-in stars (mirror the server roundtrip).
		var newCount = isAnon ? currentCount : (wasStarred ? Math.max(0, currentCount - 1) : currentCount + 1);
		rltDebug("rltToggleStar optimistic", { launchId: launchId, idx: idx, parentClass: el.parent().attr("class"), currentCount: currentCount, newCount: newCount });
		countEl.html(newCount > 0 ? newCount : "");
		if (wasStarred) {
			el.find(".launch-star-btn").html("&#9734;").attr("title", "Star this launch").css({"color":rltStarUnstarredColor(),"text-shadow":"none"});
		} else {
			el.find(".launch-star-btn").html("&#9733;").attr("title", "Unstar this launch").css({"color":"#f5c518","text-shadow":"0 0 6px rgba(245,197,24,0.6)"});
		}
	});
	if (starredFilterActive) {
		rltApplyStarredFilter();
	}

	if (typeof _paq !== "undefined") {
		_paq.push(["trackEvent", "Stars", (wasStarred ? "Unstar" : "Star") + (isAnon ? "Anon" : "SignedIn"), String(launchId)]);
	}

	if (isAnon) {
		rltLocalStarToggle(launchId);
		// Show the My Stars filter once the anon user has at least one star.
		if (!wasStarred) { $("#my-stars-filter").show(); }
		return;
	}
	if (signed_in_user === undefined) {
		// /users/me not resolved yet — treat as anon for the local mutation only.
		// On resolve, merge will reconcile.
		rltLocalStarToggle(launchId);
		return;
	}

	$.ajax({
		type: "POST",
		url: "/launches/star/" + launchId,
		contentType: "application/json",
		dataType: "json",
		success: function(resp) {
			rltDebug("rltToggleStar ajax success", { launchId: launchId, resp: resp, matchedNow: $(".launch-star[data-launch-id=" + launchId + "]").length });
			if (resp.success) {
				// Update count from server
				$(".launch-star[data-launch-id=" + launchId + "]").each(function(idx) {
					rltDebug("rltToggleStar ajax setCount", { launchId: launchId, idx: idx, parentClass: $(this).parent().attr("class"), prevText: $(this).find(".launch-star-count").text(), newCount: resp.count });
					$(this).find(".launch-star-count").html(resp.count > 0 ? resp.count : "");
				});
			}
		}
	});
}

function rltApplyStarredFilter() {
	rltDebug("rltApplyStarredFilter", { starredFilterActive: starredFilterActive, starredLaunchIds: starredLaunchIds });
	rltApplyAllFilters();
}

function rltClearStarredFilter() {
	starredFilterActive = false;
	$("#my-stars-filter").css("color", "#aaa");
	// Rebuild the launch list — updateContentFromFilter clears all filtered_launch
	// (including from the server-rendered next launch) and rebuilds the next launch box
	updateContentFromFilter($("#launch_filter"));
	rltRepositionInFeedAd();
	$("#starred-feed-link").hide();
	delete queryParameters["stars"];
	history.pushState(null, null, Object.keys(queryParameters).length ? "?" + $.param(queryParameters) : window.location.pathname);
}

$(document).ready(function() {

	$("ul.country-tabs li").click(function(event, params) {
		var country_tab = this;
		var previous_selected_country = $("[id^=country_content-]:visible");
		var country_id_selector = "#country_content-" + $(country_tab).text().toLowerCase().replace(/\s/g,"-");
		var country_providers = $(country_id_selector + " ul.company-select input[type=\"checkbox\"]");
		var country_filters = $(country_id_selector + " ul input[type=\"checkbox\"]").not("[id^=all-companies-], [id^=all-locations-]");
		var country_locations = $(country_id_selector + " ul.location-select input[type=\"checkbox\"]");

		var country_tab_checkbox = $("#country_tab_checkbox-" + $(country_tab).text().toLowerCase().replace(/\s/g,"-"));

		var selected_count = country_filters.filter(":checked").not("[id^=all-companies-], [id^=all-locations-]").length;
		var total_count = country_filters.length;

		if (previous_selected_country.attr("id") != $(country_id_selector).attr("id")) {
			$("[id^=country_content-]:visible").hide();
			$(country_id_selector).show();
		}

		if (previous_selected_country.attr("id") == $(country_id_selector).attr("id") || (params && typeof params["callback"] === "function")) {
			if (country_tab_checkbox.prop("checked")) {
				country_tab_checkbox.prop("checked", false);
				country_tab_checkbox.prop("indeterminate", false);
				country_providers.prop("checked", false);
				country_locations.prop("checked", false);
			} else if (!country_tab_checkbox.prop("checked")) {
				country_providers.prop("checked", false);
				country_locations.prop("checked", false);
				country_tab_checkbox.prop("indeterminate", false);
			}
		}

		if (!selected_count) {
			country_providers.prop("checked", true);
			//country_locations.prop("checked", true);
			country_tab_checkbox.prop("indeterminate", true);
		}		

		rltUpdateContentFromLaunchFilters();

		rltUpdateLaunchFilterCounts();
		rltRepositionInFeedAd();

		if (params && typeof params["callback"] === "function")
     		params["callback"]();

		return false;
	});

	$("[id^=country_content-] input[type=\"checkbox\"]").click(function() {
		var country_content = $(this).closest("[id^=country_content-]");
		var country_slug = $(this).closest("[id^=country_content-]").attr("id").replace("country_content-","");
		//$(this).parent().parent().parent().parent().parent().parent().attr("id").substring(16);
		var country_tab_checkbox = $("#country_tab_checkbox-" + country_slug);

		var country_providers = $("#country_content-" + country_slug + " ul.company-select input[type=\"checkbox\"]");
		var country_locations = $("#country_content-" + country_slug + " ul.location-select input[type=\"checkbox\"]");
		var country_filters = $("#country_content-" + country_slug + " ul input[type=\"checkbox\"]").not("[id^=all-companies-], [id^=all-locations-]");

		if (this.id.includes("location")) {
			if (this.id.startsWith("all-locations-")) {
				country_locations.prop("checked", this.checked);
			} else {
				var children = $(this).closest("li").find("input[type=\"checkbox\"]").not(".parent");
				if ($(this).hasClass("parent")) {
					children.prop("checked", this.checked);
				}
			}
		} else if (this.id.includes("compan")) {
			if (this.id.startsWith("all-companies-")) {
				country_providers.prop("checked", this.checked);
			}
		}

		var selected_count = country_filters.filter(":checked").not("[id^=all-]").length;
		var total_count = country_filters.length;

		if (selected_count > 0 && selected_count < total_count) {
			country_tab_checkbox.prop("indeterminate", true);
		} else if (selected_count == 0) {			
			country_tab_checkbox.prop("indeterminate", false);
			country_tab_checkbox.prop("checked", false);
		} else if (selected_count == total_count) {
			country_tab_checkbox.prop("indeterminate", false);
			country_tab_checkbox.prop("checked", true);
		}

		rltUpdateContentFromLaunchFilters();

		rltUpdateLaunchFilterCounts();
		rltRepositionInFeedAd();
	});

	$("ul.country-tabs li").each(function() {
		var this_country_slug = $(this).attr("id").replace("country_tab-","");
		var this_country = $("#" + $(this).attr("id").replace("country_tab","country_content"));
		var this_country_tab_checkbox = $("#country_tab_checkbox-" + this_country_slug);

		var selected_count = country_filters.filter(":checked").not("[id^=all-]").length;
		var total_count = country_filters.length;

		if (selected_count > 0 && selected_count < total_count) {
			this_country_tab_checkbox.prop("indeterminate", true);

			this_country_tab_checkbox.prop("indeterminate", false);

			this_country_tab_checkbox.prop("checked", true);

	$("li.tab-title a").bind("click", rltSwitchLive);

	// In-feed ad: show ad when placeholder scrolls into view (skip for premium members)
	var adEl = document.getElementById("ezoic-pub-ad-placeholder-122");
	if (adEl) {
		var adObserver = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
			entries.forEach(function(entry) {
				if (entry.isIntersecting) {
					if (signed_in_user !== undefined && (!signed_in_user || !signed_in_user.active_membership)) {
						ezstandalone.cmd.push(function() {
							ezstandalone.showAds(122);
						});
					}
					adObserver.disconnect();
				}
			});
		});
		adObserver.observe(adEl);
	}

	// --- Star buttons ---
	$(document).on("click", ".launch-star-btn", function(e) {
		e.preventDefault();
		e.stopPropagation();
		rltToggleStar($(this));
	});

	// --- Featured historical launch box ---
	$(document).on("click", ".featured-history-link", function() {
		if (typeof _paq !== "undefined") {
			_paq.push(["trackEvent", "FeaturedHistorical", "Click", String($(this).data("launch-id"))]);
		}
	});

	// Show My Stars button and init star buttons after user poll completes
	var origPollUser = rltPollUser;
	var starInitDone = false;
	var starInitInterval = setInterval(function() {
		if (signed_in_user !== undefined && !starInitDone) {
			starInitDone = true;
			clearInterval(starInitInterval);
			rltInitStarButtons();
			// Show My Stars filter for any visitor (signed-in or anon) with at least one star.
			if (starredLaunchIds.length > 0) {
				$("#my-stars-filter").show();
			}
			if (queryParameters["stars"] === "1" && !starredFilterActive) {
				if (!launches_populated) {
					launches_populated = true;
					populate_launches();
				}
				starredFilterActive = true;
				$("#my-stars-filter").css("color", "#e7a614");
				rltApplyStarredFilter();
				$("#starred-feed-link").show();
			}
		}
	}, 200);

	// My Stars filter toggle
	$("#my-stars-filter").on("click", function(e) {
		e.preventDefault();
		if (starredFilterActive) {
			rltClearStarredFilter();
		} else {
			if (!launches_populated) {
				launches_populated = true;
				populate_launches();
			}
			starredFilterActive = true;
			$(this).css("color", "#e7a614");
			rltApplyStarredFilter();
			$("#starred-feed-link").show();
			queryParameters["stars"] = "1";
			history.pushState(null, null, "?" + $.param(queryParameters));
		}
	});

});

	<script>
      $(document).foundation();

      // Close mobile menu when a link is clicked (public nav only)
      $(document).on('click', '.top-bar-container .top-bar-section ul li a', function() {
        var topbar = $(this).closest('.top-bar');
        if (topbar.hasClass('expanded')) {
          topbar.removeClass('expanded').css('height', '');
        }
      });
    </script>

    <script>
    	rltPollUser();
    </script>

    <script type="text/javascript">
		$(document).on("click", "div a.async_link, li a.async_link", function(e) {

			// Let modifier clicks open a new tab natively
			if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.shiftKey || e.which === 2) {
				return;
			}

			var dest_href = $(this).attr("href");
			var dest_title = $(this).attr("title");

			if (rltAsyncReq(dest_href, dest_title, $(this), e)) {
				e.preventDefault();
			}

		});
	</script>

	<script>
	(function() {
		var indicator = document.getElementById('offline-indicator');
		function updateStatus() {
			indicator.style.display = navigator.onLine ? 'none' : 'block';
		}
		updateStatus();
		window.addEventListener('online', function() {
			updateStatus();
			rltPollUser();
		});
		window.addEventListener('offline', updateStatus);
	})();
	</script>
